72cdef class Program: 

73 """Represent a compilation machinery to process programs into 

74 :class:`~cuda.core.ObjectCode`. 

75  

76 This object provides a unified interface to multiple underlying 

77 compiler libraries. Compilation support is enabled for a wide 

78 range of code types and compilation types. 

79  

80 Parameters 

81 ---------- 

82 code : str | bytes | bytearray 

83 The source code to compile. For C++ and PTX, must be a string. 

84 For NVVM IR, can be str, bytes, or bytearray. 

85 code_type : SourceCodeType | str 

86 The type of source code. Must be one of ``"c++"``, ``"ptx"``, or ``"nvvm"``. 

87 options : :class:`ProgramOptions`, optional 

88 Options to customize the compilation process. 

89 """ 

90 def __init__(self, code: str | bytes | bytearray, code_type: SourceCodeType | str, options: ProgramOptions | None = None):

115 def _try_materialize_nvrtc_debug_source(self, code: str) -> str | None: 

116 """Write *code* to a ``{caller}_{kernel}_XXXXXXXX.cu`` temp file for cuda-gdb. 

117  

118 ``caller`` is the Python file stem (no ``.py``). ``kernel`` is the first 

119 ``__global__`` function name, or ``kernel`` if none is found. 

120  

121 Returns None if the filesystem is not writable, so the caller can fall back 

122 to the label-only behavior instead of failing the compile. 

123 """ 

124 frame = sys._getframe() 1ghcid

125 while frame and frame.f_globals.get("__name__", "").startswith("cuda.core"): 1ghcid

126 frame = frame.f_back 

127 caller = "program" 1ghcid

128 if frame: 1ghcid

129 caller = os.path.splitext(os.path.basename(frame.f_code.co_filename))[0] 1ghcid

130 match = re.search(r"__global__.*?(\w+)\s*\(", code, re.DOTALL) 1ghcid

131 prefix = re.sub(r"\W", "_", f"{caller}_{match.group(1) if match else 'kernel'}_") 1ghcid

132 try: 1ghcid

133 fd, path = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=prefix, suffix=".cu") 2|gg h c i d

134 except OSError: 1i

135 return None 1i

136 try: 1ghcd

137 with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f: 1ghcd

138 f.write(code) 1ghcd

139 return path 1ghcd

140 except OSError: 

141 self._unlink_debug_source(path) 

142 return None

152  

153 def compile( 

154 self, 

155 target_type: ObjectCodeFormatType | str, 

156 name_expressions: tuple[str, ...] | list[str] = (), 

157 logs: object = None, 

158 *, 

159 cache: ProgramCacheResource | None = None, 

160 ) -> ObjectCode: 

161 """Compile the program to the specified target type. 

162  

163 Parameters 

164 ---------- 

165 target_type : ObjectCodeFormatType | str 

166 The compilation target. Must be one of ``"ptx"``, ``"cubin"``, or ``"ltoir"``. 

167 name_expressions : tuple | list, optional 

168 Sequence of name expressions to make accessible in the compiled code. 

169 Used for template instantiation and similar cases. 

170 logs : object, optional 

171 Object with a ``write`` method to receive compilation logs. 

172 On a cache hit no compilation runs and ``logs`` receives 

173 nothing -- callers that rely on log output to confirm a 

174 compile happened should compile without ``cache=``. 

175 cache : :class:`~cuda.core.utils.ProgramCacheResource`, optional 

176 If provided, the compiled binary is looked up in ``cache`` via a 

177 key derived from the program's code, options, and ``target_type``. 

178 On a hit the cached bytes are wrapped in a fresh 

179 :class:`~cuda.core.ObjectCode` (with the same ``target_type`` 

180 and ``ProgramOptions.name``) and returned without re-compiling; 

181 on a miss the compile output is stored as raw bytes (the cache 

182 extracts ``bytes(object_code.code)``). Passing a non-empty 

183 ``name_expressions`` together with ``cache=`` raises 

184 ``ValueError``: NVRTC populates 

185 ``ObjectCode.symbol_mapping`` at compile time and that mapping 

186 is not carried in the binary the cache stores, so cache hits 

187 would silently miss ``get_kernel(name_expression)`` lookups. 

188 Options that require an ``extra_digest`` (``include_path``, 

189 ``pre_include``, ``pch``, ``use_pch``, ``pch_dir``, NVVM 

190 ``use_libdevice=True``, or NVRTC ``options.name`` with a 

191 directory component) raise ``ValueError`` via 

192 :func:`~cuda.core.utils.make_program_cache_key`; for those 

193 compiles, use the manual ``make_program_cache_key(...)`` 

194 pattern directly. 

195  

196 ``cache=`` is independent of ``ProgramOptions.no_cache``: the 

197 former controls this program-level cache (compiled-output 

198 reuse across calls), while ``no_cache`` is forwarded to the 

199 Linker to disable its in-process JIT cache for cuLink/nvJitLink. 

200 Setting ``options.no_cache=True`` does not bypass ``cache=``, 

201 and vice-versa. 

202  

203 Returns 

204 ------- 

205 :class:`~cuda.core.ObjectCode` 

206 The compiled object code. 

207 """

236 # ``name_expressions`` is incompatible with the cache: NVRTC

239 # the binary bytes the cache stores. Without this guard the 

240 # first call (cache miss) would return an ObjectCode with 

241 # symbol_mapping populated, while every subsequent call (hit) 

242 # would return one without -- silently breaking later 

243 # ``get_kernel(name_expression)`` lookups that work on the 

244 # uncached path. Fail loud here instead.

246 raise ValueError( 2[h

247 "Program.compile(cache=...) does not support name_expressions: " 

248 "ObjectCode.symbol_mapping is populated by NVRTC at compile " 

249 "time and is not preserved across a cache round-trip, so cache " 

250 "hits would silently break get_kernel(name_expression) lookups " 

251 "that the uncached path supports. Compile without cache= when " 

252 "name_expressions are needed, or look up mangled symbols by " 

253 "hand from the cached ObjectCode."

294 @property 

295 def pch_status(self) -> PCHStatusType | None: 

296 """PCH creation outcome from the most recent :meth:`compile` call. 

297  

298 Possible values: 

299  

300 * ``"created"`` — PCH file was written successfully. 

301 * ``"not_attempted"`` — PCH creation was not attempted (e.g. the 

302 compiler decided not to, or automatic PCH processing skipped it). 

303 * ``"failed"`` — an error prevented PCH creation. 

304 * ``None`` — PCH was not requested, the program has not been 

305 compiled yet, the backend is not NVRTC (e.g. PTX or NVVM), 

306 or the NVRTC bindings are too old to report status. 

307  

308 When ``create_pch`` is set in :class:`ProgramOptions` and the PCH 

309 heap is too small, :meth:`compile` automatically resizes the heap 

310 and retries, so ``"created"`` should be the common outcome. 

311  

312 .. note:: 

313  

314 PCH is only supported for ``code_type="c++"`` programs that 

315 use the NVRTC backend. For PTX and NVVM programs this property 

316 always returns ``None``. 

317 """ 

318 if self._pch_status is None: 1f(

319 return None 1f(

320 return PCHStatusType(self._pch_status) 1f

356@dataclass 

357class ProgramOptions: 

358 """Customizable options for configuring :class:`Program`. 

359  

360 Attributes 

361 ---------- 

362 name : str, optional 

363 Name of the program. If the compilation succeeds, the name is passed down to the generated :class:`ObjectCode`. 

364 When set to `None`, ``"default_program"`` is used. 

365 arch : str, optional 

366 Pass the SM architecture value, such as ``sm_<CC>`` (for generating CUBIN) or 

367 ``compute_<CC>`` (for generating PTX). If not provided, the current device's architecture 

368 will be used. 

369 relocatable_device_code : bool, optional 

370 Enable (disable) the generation of relocatable device code. 

371 Default: False 

372 extensible_whole_program : bool, optional 

373 Do extensible whole program compilation of device code. 

374 Default: False 

375 debug : bool, optional 

376 Generate debug information. If --dopt is not specified, then turns off all optimizations. 

377 Default: False 

378 lineinfo: bool, optional 

379 Generate line-number information. 

380 Default: False 

381 device_code_optimize : bool, optional 

382 Enable device code optimization. When specified along with '-G', enables limited debug information generation 

383 for optimized device code. 

384 Default: None 

385 ptxas_options : str | list[str], optional 

386 Specify one or more options directly to ptxas, the PTX optimizing assembler. Options should be strings. 

387 For example ["-v", "-O2"]. 

388 Default: None 

389 max_register_count : int, optional 

390 Specify the maximum amount of registers that GPU functions can use. 

391 Default: None 

392 ftz : bool, optional 

393 When performing single-precision floating-point operations, flush denormal values to zero or preserve denormal 

394 values. 

395 Default: False 

396 prec_sqrt : bool, optional 

397 For single-precision floating-point square root, use IEEE round-to-nearest mode or use a faster approximation. 

398 Default: True 

399 prec_div : bool, optional 

400 For single-precision floating-point division and reciprocals, use IEEE round-to-nearest mode or use a faster 

401 approximation. 

402 Default: True 

403 fma : bool, optional 

404 Enables (disables) the contraction of floating-point multiplies and adds/subtracts into floating-point 

405 multiply-add operations. 

406 Default: True 

407 use_fast_math : bool, optional 

408 Make use of fast math operations. 

409 Default: False 

410 extra_device_vectorization : bool, optional 

411 Enables more aggressive device code vectorization in the NVVM optimizer. 

412 Default: False 

413 link_time_optimization : bool, optional 

414 Generate intermediate code for later link-time optimization. 

415 Default: False 

416 gen_opt_lto : bool, optional 

417 Run the optimizer passes before generating the LTO IR. 

418 Default: False 

419 define_macro : str | tuple[str, str] | list[str | tuple[str, str]], optional 

420 Predefine a macro. Can be either a string, in which case that macro will be set to 1, a 2 element tuple of 

421 strings, in which case the first element is defined as the second, or a list of strings or tuples. 

422 Default: None 

423 undefine_macro : str | list[str], optional 

424 Cancel any previous definition of a macro, or list of macros. 

425 Default: None 

426 include_path : str | list[str], optional 

427 Add the directory or directories to the list of directories to be searched for headers. 

428 Default: None 

429 pre_include : str | list[str], optional 

430 Preinclude one or more headers during preprocessing. Can be either a string or a list of strings. 

431 Default: None 

432 no_source_include : bool, optional 

433 Disable the default behavior of adding the directory of each input source to the include path. 

434 Default: False 

435 std : str, optional 

436 Set language dialect to C++03, C++11, C++14, C++17 or C++20. 

437 Default: c++17 

438 builtin_move_forward : bool, optional 

439 Provide builtin definitions of std::move and std::forward. 

440 Default: True 

441 builtin_initializer_list : bool, optional 

442 Provide builtin definitions of std::initializer_list class and member functions. 

443 Default: True 

444 disable_warnings : bool, optional 

445 Inhibit all warning messages. 

446 Default: False 

447 restrict : bool, optional 

448 Programmer assertion that all kernel pointer parameters are restrict pointers. 

449 Default: False 

450 device_as_default_execution_space : bool, optional 

451 Treat entities with no execution space annotation as __device__ entities. 

452 Default: False 

453 device_int128 : bool, optional 

454 Allow the __int128 type in device code. 

455 Default: False 

456 optimization_info : str, optional 

457 Provide optimization reports for the specified kind of optimization. 

458 Default: None 

459 no_display_error_number : bool, optional 

460 Disable the display of a diagnostic number for warning messages. 

461 Default: False 

462 diag_error : int | list[int], optional 

463 Emit error for a specified diagnostic message number or comma-separated list of numbers. 

464 Default: None 

465 diag_suppress : int | list[int], optional 

466 Suppress a specified diagnostic message number or comma-separated list of numbers. 

467 Default: None 

468 diag_warn : int | list[int], optional 

469 Emit warning for a specified diagnostic message number or comma-separated list of numbers. 

470 Default: None 

471 brief_diagnostics : bool, optional 

472 Disable or enable showing source line and column info in a diagnostic. 

473 Default: False 

474 time : str, optional 

475 Generate a CSV table with the time taken by each compilation phase. 

476 Default: None 

477 split_compile : int, optional 

478 Perform compiler optimizations in parallel. 

479 Default: 1 

480 fdevice_syntax_only : bool, optional 

481 Ends device compilation after front-end syntax checking. 

482 Default: False 

483 minimal : bool, optional 

484 Omit certain language features to reduce compile time for small programs. 

485 Default: False 

486 no_cache : bool, optional 

487 Disable compiler caching. 

488 Default: False 

489 fdevice_time_trace : str, optional 

490 Generate time trace JSON for profiling compilation (NVRTC only). 

491 Default: None 

492 device_float128 : bool, optional 

493 Allow __float128 type in device code (NVRTC only). 

494 Default: False 

495 frandom_seed : str, optional 

496 Set random seed for randomized optimizations (NVRTC only). 

497 Default: None 

498 ofast_compile : str, optional 

499 Fast compilation mode: "0", "min", "mid", or "max" (NVRTC only). 

500 Default: None 

501 pch : bool, optional 

502 Use default precompiled header (NVRTC only, CUDA 12.8+). 

503 Default: False 

504 create_pch : str, optional 

505 Create precompiled header file (NVRTC only, CUDA 12.8+). 

506 Default: None 

507 use_pch : str, optional 

508 Use specific precompiled header file (NVRTC only, CUDA 12.8+). 

509 Default: None 

510 pch_dir : str, optional 

511 PCH directory location (NVRTC only, CUDA 12.8+). 

512 Default: None 

513 pch_verbose : bool, optional 

514 Verbose PCH output (NVRTC only, CUDA 12.8+). 

515 Default: False 

516 pch_messages : bool, optional 

517 Control PCH diagnostic messages (NVRTC only, CUDA 12.8+). 

518 Default: False 

519 instantiate_templates_in_pch : bool, optional 

520 Control template instantiation in PCH (NVRTC only, CUDA 12.8+). 

521 Default: False 

522 extra_sources : list of 2-tuples or tuple of 2-tuples, optional 

523 Additional NVVM IR modules to compile together with the main program, specified as 

524 ``((name1, source1), (name2, source2), ...)``. Each name is a string identifier used 

525 in diagnostic messages. Each source can be a string (textual LLVM IR) or bytes/bytearray 

526 (LLVM bitcode). Only supported for the NVVM backend. 

527 Default: None 

528 use_libdevice : bool, optional 

529 Load NVIDIA's `libdevice <https://docs.nvidia.com/cuda/libdevice-users-guide/>`_ 

530 math builtins library. Only supported for the NVVM backend. 

531 Default: False 

532 numba_debug : bool, optional 

533 Emit the debug information layout expected by Numba. Recognized only by 

534 newer toolkits; compilers that do not support it reject the option with 

535 an error. Applies only to the NVVM and NVRTC compilation backends -- 

536 ``code_type="ptx"`` is processed by the linker, which cannot honor it, 

537 so enabling this option there emits a :class:`UserWarning` and the 

538 option is ignored. 

539 Default: None 

540 """

637 def as_bytes(self, backend: CompilerBackendType | str, target_type: ObjectCodeFormatType | str | None = None) -> list[bytes]: 

638 """Convert program options to bytes format for the specified backend. 

639  

640 This method transforms the program options into a format suitable for the 

641 specified compiler backend. Different backends may use different option names 

642 and formats even for the same conceptual options. 

643  

644 Parameters 

645 ---------- 

646 backend : CompilerBackendType | str 

647 The compiler backend to prepare options for. Must be either "nvrtc" or "nvvm". 

648 target_type : ObjectCodeFormatType | str, optional 

649 The compilation target type (e.g., "ptx", "cubin", "ltoir"). Some backends 

650 require additional options based on the target type. 

651  

652 Returns 

653 ------- 

654 list[bytes] 

655 List of option strings encoded as bytes. 

656  

657 Raises 

658 ------ 

659 ValueError 

660 If an unknown backend is specified. 

661 CUDAError 

662 If an option incompatible with the specified backend is set. 

663  

664 Examples 

665 -------- 

666 >>> options = ProgramOptions(arch="sm_80", debug=True) 

667 >>> nvrtc_options = options.as_bytes("nvrtc") 

668 """

799cdef inline object _translate_program_options(object options): 

800 """Translate ProgramOptions to LinkerOptions for PTX compilation.""" 

801 # ``numba_debug`` is an NVVM/NVRTC compiler option that no linking backend can 

802 # honor. It used to be forwarded into ``LinkerOptions`` and dropped without a 

803 # word; warn instead, and do not forward -- forwarding would only trigger the 

804 # deprecation warning on a field the user never touched. ``UserWarning``, not 

805 # ``DeprecationWarning``: ``ProgramOptions.numba_debug`` is not deprecated, it 

806 # is fully supported on NVVM and NVRTC and merely inapplicable here. The gate 

807 # is truthiness, matching ``_prepare_nvvm_options_impl``: only an enabled 

808 # ``numba_debug`` asks for something this path cannot deliver. 

809 if options.numba_debug: 2.hChDhhhjhkhlhmhnhohphqhrh

810 warn( 2hh

811 "numba_debug is ignored for code_type='ptx', which is processed by the linker; " 

812 "it applies only to the NVVM and NVRTC compilation backends.", 

813 UserWarning, 2hh

814 stacklevel=4, 

815 ) 

816 return LinkerOptions( 2.hChDhhhjhkhlhmhnhohphqhrh

817 name=options.name, 2.hChDhhhjhkhlhmhnhohphqhrh

818 arch=options.arch, 2.hChDhhhjhkhlhmhnhohphqhrh

819 max_register_count=options.max_register_count, 2.hChDhhhjhkhlhmhnhohphqhrh

820 time=options.time, 2.hChDhhhjhkhlhmhnhohphqhrh

821 link_time_optimization=options.link_time_optimization, 2.hChDhhhjhkhlhmhnhohphqhrh

822 debug=options.debug, 2.hChDhhhjhkhlhmhnhohphqhrh

823 lineinfo=options.lineinfo, 2.hChDhhhjhkhlhmhnhohphqhrh

824 ftz=options.ftz, 2.hChDhhhjhkhlhmhnhohphqhrh

825 prec_div=options.prec_div, 2.hChDhhhjhkhlhmhnhohphqhrh

826 prec_sqrt=options.prec_sqrt, 2.hChDhhhjhkhlhmhnhohphqhrh

827 fma=options.fma, 2.hChDhhhjhkhlhmhnhohphqhrh

828 split_compile=options.split_compile, 2.hChDhhhjhkhlhmhnhohphqhrh

829 ptxas_options=options.ptxas_options, 2.hChDhhhjhkhlhmhnhohphqhrh

830 no_cache=options.no_cache, 2.hChDhhhjhkhlhmhnhohphqhrh

831 )

1021cdef object _read_pch_status(cynvrtc.nvrtcProgram prog): 

1022 """Query nvrtcGetPCHCreateStatus and translate to a high-level string.""" 

1023 cdef cynvrtc.nvrtcResult err 

1024 with nogil: 1fep

1025 err = cynvrtc.nvrtcGetPCHCreateStatus(prog) 1fep

1026 if err == cynvrtc.nvrtcResult.NVRTC_SUCCESS: 1fep

1027 return PCHStatusType.CREATED 1fep

1028 if err == cynvrtc.nvrtcResult.NVRTC_ERROR_PCH_CREATE_HEAP_EXHAUSTED: 

1029 return None # sentinel: caller should auto-retry 

1030 if err == cynvrtc.nvrtcResult.NVRTC_ERROR_NO_PCH_CREATE_ATTEMPTED: 

1031 return PCHStatusType.NOT_ATTEMPTED 

1032 return PCHStatusType.FAILED

1314cpdef void _assert_single_dashed_nvvm_options(options: list[str]) except *: 

1315 """Guard against emitting a double-dashed option to libNVVM. 

1316  

1317 libNVVM's parser accepts only single-dashed options and rejects the 

1318 double-dashed spelling of every option with NVVM_ERROR_INVALID_OPTION 

1319 (see #2570). Every option on this path is generated from typed fields, so 

1320 a double dash can only mean a bug in ``cuda.core`` rather than bad user 

1321 input. Fail here, naming the option, instead of leaving the user with 

1322 libNVVM's opaque error. 

1323 """ 

1324 for option in options: 24e5e`gNi^g1eNfSfOfTfPfUf2e7eQfVf3e8eRfWfghsha b ihthuhvhwhxhMhNhOhyhzhAh

1325 if option.startswith("--"): 24e5e`gNi^g1eNfSfOfTfPfUf2e7eQfVf3e8eRfWfghsha b ihthuhvhwhxhMhNhOhyhzhAh

1326 raise RuntimeError( 2Ni

1327 f"Internal error: NVVM option {option!r} is double-dashed. libNVVM accepts " 2Ni

1328 f"only single-dashed options; emit {option[1:]!r} instead." 2Ni

1329 ) 

1330
